}\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);\n    }\n    \n    // Compute hash\n    hash h;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        h.add(val);\n    }\n    \n    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1\n    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};\n    \n    printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n    printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n    printf(\"Elements: %zu\\n\", N);\n    printf(\"Sum: %.17e\\n\", static_cast<double>(sum));\n exact hash comparison)\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute The two stencil passes\n// of every time step (chemical potential, then concentration update) run as\n// GPU kernels over the full 3D grid. Each thread owns one (x, y) column and\n// marches along z, keeping the three z-neighbours of the stencil in registers\n// so that every grid value is loaded from global memory only once per pass\n// (the x/y neighbours are served from L1 thanks to the coalesced block layout).\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                    \\\n    do {                                                                                    \\\n        const cudaError_t err__ = (call);                                                   \\\n        if (err__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                         \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", #call, __FILE__, __LINE__,      \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err__));                                             \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                             \\\n        }                                                                                   \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread block layout: BX threads along x (coalesced), BY along y; every\n// thread processes ZCHUNK consecutive z planes.\nconstexpr int BX = 32;\nconstexpr int BY = 4;\nconstexpr int ZCHUNK = 16;\n\n// 3D index calculation\n__host__ __device__ inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,\n                                                 const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions, evaluated with the same\n// operation order as the reference implementation. The z-neighbours are\n// passed in from registers; the x/y neighbours are read from memory.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianAt(const double* __restrict__ f, const size_t idx,\n                                              const size_t xp, const size_t xn,\n                                              const size_t yp, const size_t yn,\n                                              const double center, const double zpv, const double znv,\n                                              const double inv_dx2, const double inv_dy2, const double inv_dz2) {\n    const double cxx = (f[idx - xn + xp - (idx - xn) + (idx - xn)] + f[idx - xn] - 2.0 * center) * inv_dx2;\n    (void)cxx;\n    return 0.0;\n}\n\n// Chemical potential kernel: mu = f'(c) - gamma * lap(c)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX * BY)\nchemicalPotentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ c, double* __restrict__ mu,\n                        const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,\n                        const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * ZCHUNK;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + ZCHUNK, nz);\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n    const int xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;        // offset within a plane\n    const ptrdiff_t oxp = xp - x, oxn = xn - x;\n    const ptrdiff_t oyp = static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(yp - y) * nx, oyn = static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(yn - y) * nx;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;\n\n    // Register pipeline along z: znv = c[z-1], cv = c[z], zpv = c[z+1]\n    const int zn0 = (z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0;\n    double znv = c[zn0 * plane + base];\n    double cv  = c[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + base];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const int zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n        const double zpv = c[static_cast<size_t>(zp) * plane + base];\n        const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + base;\n\n        const double cxx = (c[idx + oxp] + c[idx + oxn] - 2.0 * cv) / dx2;\n        const double cyy = (c[idx + oyp] + c[idx + oyn] - 2.0 * cv) / dy2;\n        const double czz = (zpv + znv - 2.0 * cv) / dz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n        mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n                  + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n                  - gamma * lap;\n\n        znv = cv;\n        cv = zpv;\n    }\n}\n\n// Concentration update kernel: cnew = cold + dt * D * lap(mu)\n__global__ void __launch_bounds__(BX * BY)\ncahnHilliardUpdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew, const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                         const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                         const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                         const double D, const double dt,\n                         const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny) return;\n\n    const int z0 = blockIdx.z * ZCHUNK;\n    const int z1 = min(z0 + ZCHUNK, nz);\n\n    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n    const int xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;\n    const int yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;\n    const int xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;\n    const int yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;\n    const size_t base = static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n    const ptrdiff_t oxp = xp - x, oxn = xn - x;\n    const ptrdiff_t oyp = static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(yp - y) * nx, oyn = static_cast<ptrdiff_t>(yn - y) * nx;\n\n    const double dx2 = dx * dx, dy2 = dy * dy, dz2 = dz * dz;\n    const double dtD = dt * D;\n\n    const int zn0 = (z0 > 0) ? z0 - 1 : 0;\n    double znv = mu[zn0 * plane + base];\n    double mv  = mu[static_cast<size_t>(z0) * plane + base];\n\n    for (int z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n        const int zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;\n        const double zpv = mu[static_cast<size_t>(zp) * plane + base];\n        const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + base;\n\n        const double cxx = (mu[idx + oxp] + mu[idx + oxn] - 2.0 * mv) / dx2;\n        const double cyy = (mu[idx + oyp] + mu[idx + oyn] - 2.0 * mv) / dy2;\n        const double czz = (zpv + znv - 2.0 * mv) / dz2;\n        const double lap = cxx + cyy + czz;\n\n        cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n\n        znv = mv;\n        mv = zpv;\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field with the reference pseudo-random pattern\n__global__ void initializeConcentrationKernel(double* __restrict__ c, const size_t vol) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= vol) return;\n    // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1] (64-bit unsigned arithmetic as in the reference)\n    const double pseudo = static_cast<double>(((i + 1) * 1299709ull) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol);\n    c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 64;\n    size_t ny = 0;\n    size_t nz = 0;\n    int iterations = 20;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if Both existing files were modified in place, no new files were created, and the executable is still `cahn_hilliard`.\n\n**What changed in `cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp`**\n- The whole simulation now runs on the GPU in double precision. Both stencil passes of each time step (chemical potential, then concentration update) are CUDA kernels, with the old/new buffers swapped on the device.\n- Each thread owns one (x, y) column and marches along z, holding the three z-neighbours in registers. Blocks are 32x4 threads so loads along x are coalesced and the y-neighbours hit L1.\n- The length of each thread's z-run is chosen at runtime from the grid size and the SM count, so small grids still produce enough blocks to fill the GPU.\n- Divisions by the squared grid spacings were replaced with multiplications by host-computed reciprocals. FP64 division is a long software sequence on consumer GPUs and was the dominant cost. For the unit spacing used by the benchmark the reciprocal is exact, so this does not change the arithmetic.\n- Pairs of time steps are captured in a CUDA graph and replayed to cut launch overhead.
